# GolemSculpt tooltips in Chinese (Simplified) (zh).
#
# Each tooltip is a pair of lines: "en:" is the English the app has, "zh:"
# is what the tooltip shows instead. To fix a translation, edit its
# "zh:" line. Words in {braces} are filled in by the app and must stay;
# \n is a line break. Names of buttons and cards stay in English, as they
# are on screen. tools/translations.py adds what is new.
#
# The words used throughout:
#   mesh = 网格
#   vertex / vertices = 顶点
#   triangle = 三角面
#   quad = 四边面
#   face group = 面组
#   group = 组
#   brush = 笔刷
#   brush ring (the ring under the brush) = 笔刷圈
#   stroke = 笔触
#   dab = 笔点
#   alpha = Alpha 图
#   matcap = Matcap
#   falloff = 衰减
#   strength = 强度
#   radius = 半径
#   smooth = 平滑
#   inflate = 膨胀
#   crease = 折痕
#   flatten = 压平
#   pinch = 收缩
#   mask = 遮罩
#   hide / show = 隐藏 / 显示
#   symmetry = 对称
#   mirror = 镜像
#   radial symmetry = 径向对称
#   subdivision level = 细分级别
#   finest / coarsest level = 最高级别 / 最低级别
#   remesh = Remesh（作命令名）/ 重构网格（泛指）
#   decimate = Decimate（作命令名）/ 减面（泛指）
#   layer / sculpt layer / paint layer = 图层 / 雕刻图层 / 绘制图层
#   bake = 烘焙
#   paint (verb / noun) = 绘制 / 颜料
#   material = 材质
#   pivot = 轴心
#   gizmo = Gizmo
#   viewport = 视口
#   card / panel / tab = 卡片 / 面板 / 选项卡
#   object / model / scene = 物体 / 模型 / 场景
#   undo / redo / undo step = 撤销 / 重做 / 撤销步骤
#   orbit / turntable = 环绕视图 / 转盘
#   pen pressure = 压感
#   status line = 状态栏
#   recovery copy = 恢复副本
#   instance / linked copy = 实例 / 关联副本
#   array = 阵列
#   boolean / voxel / resolution = 布尔 / 体素 / 分辨率
#   profile (Lathe) = 轮廓线
#   peg / socket / joint (Keys) = 销钉 / 销孔 / 榫接点
#   click / double-click / right-click / drag / hold / tap = 点击 / 双击 / 右键点击 / 拖动 / 按住 / 轻点
#   tick / untick = 勾选 / 取消勾选

en: Deleted "{name}". Undo (Ctrl+Z) restores it.
zh: 已删除“{name}”。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可恢复。

en: The last object cannot be deleted.
zh: 最后一个物体无法删除。

en: Copy added on top of the original. Move it with Position.
zh: 已在原物体上方添加副本。用 Position 移动它。

en: Instance added on top of the original. Move it with Position. Sculpting either changes both.
zh: 已在原物体上方添加实例。用 Position 移动它。雕刻其中任何一个，两者都会改变。

en: The model's UVs cover none of the picture: they are all outside the square from 0 to 1, or have no area.
zh: 模型的 UV 没有覆盖图片的任何部分：它们要么全在 0 到 1 的方形之外，要么没有面积。

en: With Mirror {planes} on, its sides are not mirror images of each other (up to {apart} apart), so it was decimated as it is: each side keeps its own shape, and their points do not pair up.
zh: Mirror {planes} 已开启，但它的两侧并不互为镜像（最多相差 {apart}），因此按原样减面：每一侧保持各自的形状，两侧的顶点也不再一一对应。

en: Its sides mirror each other across {planes}, point for point.
zh: 它的两侧沿 {planes} 互为镜像，逐点对应。

en: Its sides mirror each other across {planes}, triangle for triangle; where they were not alike (up to {apart} apart), each kept its own shape.
zh: 它的两侧沿 {planes} 互为镜像，逐个三角面对应；不一致的地方（最多相差 {apart}）各自保持原有形状。

en: At {0} points the mirrored triangles could not follow their own side without folding over: those are up to {apart} from it.
zh: 有 {0} 处，镜像的三角面若跟随自己的一侧就会翻折，因此没有跟随：它们与本侧最多相距 {apart}。

zh: 添加你自己的灰度图片（.png 或 .jpg）。它会被复制到 alpha 文件夹。

en: Drag to Place (Off)
zh: Drag to Place (Off)（拖动放置）

en: On: one stroke is one print. Press where its middle goes and drag outward to size it; lift to leave it. Strength sets how tall it stands for its size. Off: the picture is laid down all along the stroke.
zh: 开：一笔就是一个印记。在印记中心的位置按下并向外拖动来调整大小；抬起即放下。Strength 决定它相对于大小的高度。关：图片沿整条笔触铺开。

en: How big the picture is against the brush ring. At 100% it fills the square the ring sits in.
zh: 图片相对于笔刷圈的大小。100% 时它会填满笔刷圈所在的方框。

en: Turns each print by a random angle, up to this far either way, so a row of prints does not look stamped by a machine. At 180° a print may face any way at all. Raise the brush's Spacing to see the prints apart. 0 is off.
zh: 把每个印记随机旋转一个角度，两个方向最多转这么多，让一排印记不会像机器盖出来的。180° 时印记可以朝向任意方向。调高笔刷的 Spacing 可以把印记分开看。0 为关闭。

en: Turn With Stroke (Off)
zh: Turn With Stroke (Off)（随笔触旋转）

en: On: the top of the picture points the way the stroke is going, so a rake's lines follow your hand (or, with Drag to Place, the way you drag). Off: it points up the screen.
zh: 开：图片的顶部指向笔触前进的方向，所以齿耙的线条会跟着你的手走（使用 Drag to Place 时则跟着拖动的方向）。关：图片顶部指向屏幕上方。

en: Repeat (Off)
zh: Repeat (Off)（重复）

en: Repeat the picture past its edges. With a small Alpha Size this covers the brush in a fine pattern.
zh: 让图片越过边缘重复。Alpha Size 较小时，这会用细密的图案铺满笔刷。

en: Invert (Off)
zh: Invert (Off)（反转）

en: Swap black and white: bumps become pits.
zh: 黑白互换：凸起变成凹坑。

en: The brush's soft edge still fades the picture toward the ring: slide Focal Shift toward Hard to print more of it. A stroke smears a picture along itself; Drag to Place prints it once, whole.
zh: 笔刷的软边仍会让图片向笔刷圈边缘淡出：把 Focal Shift 滑向 Hard 可印出更多部分。普通笔触会沿自身把图片抹开；Drag to Place 则把图片完整印一次。

en: Not a picture this app reads: a matcap must be a .png or .jpg.
zh: 不是本应用能读取的图片：Matcap 必须是 .png 或 .jpg。

en: {count} matcaps{change}. There is no matcap folder yet; Import Matcap makes one.
zh: {count} 个 Matcap{change}。还没有 matcap 文件夹；Import Matcap 会创建一个。

en: Could not find where to keep matcaps.
zh: 找不到存放 Matcap 的位置。

en: UVs lay a model's surface out flat in a square, so pictures (maps) can be painted on it. For a game or a render: Clone the sculpt, Quad Remesh the copy, Unwrap UVs on it, then Bake Maps from the sculpt and export the copy with the maps.
zh: UV 把模型的表面平铺展开到一个方形里，这样就能在上面绘制图片（贴图）。用于游戏或渲染：先 Clone 雕刻模型，对副本做 Quad Remesh，再对副本执行 Unwrap UVs，然后从雕刻模型 Bake Maps，并把副本连同贴图一起导出。

en: This object has UVs. Unwrapping again makes new ones.
zh: 这个物体有 UV。再次展开会生成新的 UV。

en: This object has no UVs.
zh: 这个物体没有 UV。

en: Cut the UVs along the face groups' borders, so the seams go where you drew them. Without face groups the unwrap places the seams itself.
zh: 沿着面组的边界切开 UV，让接缝落在你画好的位置。没有面组时，展开会自己决定接缝的位置。

en: Lays the selected object out flat in the square: UVs for baking and painting maps, kept when it is exported as OBJ or FBX. The model does not move. One undo step (Ctrl+Z).
zh: 把所选物体平铺展开到方形里：生成用于烘焙和绘制贴图的 UV，导出为 OBJ 或 FBX 时会一并保留。模型不会移动。占一个撤销步骤（Ctrl+Z）。

en: Needs the sculpt to bake from as a second object, where this one is.
zh: 需要在这个物体所在的位置有第二个物体，作为烘焙来源的雕刻模型。

en: Maps {size} pixels square.

en: The sculpt's detail as a tangent-space normal map (OpenGL, Y up), as Blender, Unity and Godot read it.
zh: 雕刻模型的细节，生成为切线空间法线贴图（OpenGL，Y up），与 Blender、Unity、Godot 读取的格式一致。

en: How far the sculpt stands off this surface: a 32-bit EXR, a 16-bit PNG and a note of the range.

en: How much light reaches each place on the sculpt. The slow one: seconds for a 2K map.

en: Tap a brush to take it up; the number keys pick the ones with a number. Every brush keeps its own settings, and changes stick as you make them.\n\nRight-click a brush (or press … at the top for the brush in hand) to put it on the Space wheel or take it off, duplicate it as a brush of your own, reset it, delete it or export it. en: Sets Target Triangles to {label} of the triangles the selected object has now.
zh: 把 Target Triangles 设为所选物体当前三角面数的 {label}。

en: Shows the model decimated before anything is changed. Drag Target Triangles and it follows at once; Apply (Enter) keeps what is shown, Cancel (Esc) leaves the model as it was. On a large model it takes a few seconds to work out first.
zh: 在做出任何改动之前，先显示减面后的模型。拖动 Target Triangles，预览会立即跟随；Apply（Enter）保留当前显示的结果，Cancel（Esc）让模型保持原样。模型较大时，需要先计算几秒钟。

en: Takes triangles off and keeps the shape, for a game model that has to fit a budget (200k to 500k triangles, say). Brings the selected object down to about Target Triangles, taking them from where the surface is flat and keeping them where it bends. A closed model stays closed. With a mirror on, the sides come out mirror images of each other, point for point. Fully masked surface is left as it is, and a softer mask keeps more of its detail the stronger it is; hidden parts are decimated too and stay hidden. It works in the background on a large model, with its progress and a Cancel at the bottom of the view. Undo (Ctrl+Z) brings the triangles back.
zh: 减少三角面而保持形状，适合必须符合预算的游戏模型（比如 20 万到 50 万个三角面）。把所选物体减到大约 Target Triangles 的数量，从表面平坦的地方拿走三角面，在弯曲的地方保留。封闭的模型仍然封闭。开启镜像时，两侧会逐点互为镜像。完全被遮罩的表面保持原样，较软的遮罩越强，保留的细节越多；隐藏的部分同样会被减面，并保持隐藏。模型较大时它在后台运行，进度和 Cancel 按钮显示在视图底部。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可找回这些三角面。

en: Adds triangles under the brush as you sculpt, so detail appears where you work, and merges them where a bigger brush asks for less. Standard, Clay, Crease, Inflate, Flatten and Layer do it; Move, Pose, Snake Hook and Smooth don't. Remesh evens the whole model out again.
zh: 雕刻时在笔刷下增加三角面，让细节出现在你工作的位置，并在更大的笔刷需要更少三角面的地方将其合并。Standard、Clay、Crease、Inflate、Flatten 和 Layer 支持这样做；Move、Pose、Snake Hook 和 Smooth 不支持。Remesh 可以让整个模型重新变均匀。

en: Longest edge left under the brush, as a percentage of the brush radius: lower is finer. A smaller brush adds finer detail. With Both or Remove, edges under 40% of it are merged.
zh: 笔刷下保留的最长边长度，以笔刷半径的百分比计：越低越精细。笔刷越小，添加的细节越精细。选择 Both 或 Remove 时，短于它 40% 的边会被合并。

en: Regions of the surface, each with a colour of its own, to hide, mask and sculpt one at a time. en: Tints every face with the colour of its group, over whatever material is showing. A group keeps its colour for good; surface in no group keeps the material's own colour.

en: Everything that shows becomes a new group: hide what should stay out first. With nothing hidden the whole object becomes one group.

zh: Group Masked 会保留遮罩，而不是清除它。

en: Each separate part of the object becomes a group of its own: the parts a Join brought together, or shells that only touch. One connected piece is one group.
zh: 物体的每个独立部分各成为一个组：包括由 Join 合在一起的部分，或只是相互接触的壳体。连在一起的一整块就是一个组。

en: Splits the object into groups along its sharp edges, where the surface turns by more than the angle below. For hard shapes; on a soft sculpt a low angle makes a great many small groups.
zh: 沿尖锐的边把物体分成若干组，也就是表面转折超过下面角度的地方。适用于硬朗的形状；在柔和的雕刻上，角度设低了会产生大量小组。

en: Then click a group on the model to pick it, for the buttons below. Ctrl + Shift + click on the model picks a group and isolates it at once, without this button.
zh: 然后在模型上点击一个组来选取它，供下面的按钮使用。在模型上按 Ctrl + Shift + 点击，无需这个按钮，就能直接选取并孤立一个组。

en: Then click another group on the model: the group picked becomes part of it.
zh: 然后在模型上点击另一个组：已选取的组会并入它。

en: Merge Into...
zh: Merge Into...（并入）

en: Hides everything but the group picked, so it can be worked on alone. Press again, or Show All, to bring the rest back. Ctrl + Shift + click on the model does the same.

en: Hides the group picked. Show All brings it back.
zh: 隐藏所选组。Show All 可恢复。

zh: 重新显示这个物体所有被隐藏的面。

zh: 遮罩所选组并取消其余部分的遮罩：笔刷之后就不会碰它。

en: Masks everything but the group picked, so brushes work on it alone. The mask can be blurred for a soft edge, which Stay in Group cannot give.
zh: 遮罩所选组之外的一切，让笔刷只作用于它。遮罩可以模糊出软边，这是 Stay in Group 做不到的。

zh: 点击要并入的那个组。

en: Stay in Group (Off)
zh: Stay in Group (Off)（限制在组内）

en: A stroke changes only the face group it begins on, and stops dead at the group's border. With symmetry, the stroke is kept to the groups its sides begin on. Does nothing on an object without groups (Mesh tab, Face Groups).
zh: 笔触只改变它起笔所在的面组，并在该组的边界处戛然而止。开启对称时，笔触各侧会被限制在各自起笔所在的组内。对没有组的物体不起作用（Mesh 选项卡，Face Groups）。

en: While this tool is up, Ctrl+Z steps through its own history first; the scene's history list is here again once it is put away.
zh: 此工具打开期间，Ctrl+Z 会先逐步撤销它自己的历史；收起工具后，这里会重新显示场景的历史列表。

en: History: every step Undo would take back, by name. Tap one to go back to just before it.
zh: 历史记录：撤销会收回的每一步，按名称列出。轻点其中一步可回到它之前。

en: Passes of sculpting kept apart from the model. Each can be turned down, hidden or deleted without touching the rest. The red dot marks the layer that records what you sculpt; with no dot lit you sculpt the model underneath, and the layers ride on it.\n\nRemesh, Decimate, Join, a boolean and the other commands that rebuild the mesh merge the layers into the model first. Undo (Ctrl+Z) brings them back.
zh: 与模型分开保存的几遍雕刻。每个图层都可以调低、隐藏或删除，不影响其他部分。红点标记记录你雕刻内容的图层；没有亮起的红点时，你雕刻的是下面的模型，图层叠在它之上。\n\nRemesh、Decimate、Join、布尔运算以及其他会重建网格的命令，会先把图层合并进模型。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可找回它们。

en: Makes every layer part of the model as it shows now, and empties the list: the shape does not change. A layer that is turned down is baked turned down, and a hidden one adds nothing. Undo (Ctrl+Z) brings them back.
zh: 把每个图层按当前显示的效果变成模型的一部分，并清空列表：形状不会改变。调低的图层按调低后的效果烘焙，隐藏的图层不会添加任何内容。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可找回它们。

en: Adds an empty layer and starts recording on it: what you sculpt from now on goes into the layer, and the model underneath is left as it is. Undo (Ctrl+Z) removes it.
zh: 添加一个空图层并开始在其上记录：之后雕刻的内容都会进入这个图层，下面的模型保持原样。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可移除它。

en: An object can have {MOST_LAYERS} layers. Bake the ones that are finished.
zh: 一个物体最多可以有 {MOST_LAYERS} 个图层。请把已完成的图层烘焙掉。

en: On a lower subdivision level this waits for the finest one (Page Up).
zh: 在较低的细分级别上，它要等到最高级别才会生效（Page Up）。

en: This layer is recording: what you sculpt goes into it. Click to stop, and sculpt the model underneath instead.

en: Click to record on this layer: what you sculpt then goes into it and nowhere else. One layer records at a time.

en: How much of this layer is in the shape: 100% as it was sculpted, 0% none of it. Drag to try the sculpt stronger or fainter; nothing is lost at any setting. One drag is one undo step.
zh: 这个图层在形状中占多少：100% 是雕刻时的原样，0% 则完全不起作用。拖动可尝试让雕刻更强或更淡；任何设置下都不会丢失内容。一次拖动算一个撤销步骤。

en: No layer is recording: you are sculpting the model underneath.
zh: 没有图层在记录：你正在雕刻下面的模型。

en: Levels of the same model, each with four times the triangles of the one below. Shape the big forms on a low level and the detail on a high one: what is sculpted on one is kept on the others.\n\nA big change made on a low level comes back up with the fine detail riding on it. One point pulled out by itself on a low level comes back up as a softer, lower swelling: the finer levels round it off.
zh: 同一个模型的多个级别，每一级的三角面数是下一级的四倍。在低级别上塑造大形，在高级别上雕刻细节：在某一级上雕刻的内容会保留到其他级别。\n\n在低级别上做的大改动，回到高级别时会带着上面的精细细节。在低级别上单独拉出的一个点，回到高级别时会变成更柔和、更低的隆起：更精细的级别会把它磨圆。

en: This model is quads on every level, and export writes the quads of the level it is on.

en: Adds a level with four times the triangles above the finest one and moves to it (Ctrl + D). The model is rounded a little as it is divided, as clay is when it is smoothed; tick Flat to keep every point where it is. A quad remesh divides into quads. Undo (Ctrl+Z) takes the level away again.
zh: 在最高级别之上添加三角面数为其四倍的一级并切换到它（Ctrl + D）。模型在细分时会略微变圆，就像泥被抹平一样；勾选 Flat 可让每个点保持原位。经过 Quad Remesh 的模型会按四边面细分。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可再次移除这一级。

en: Subdivide only cuts each face into four and moves nothing: for hard-edged shapes that must keep their flat faces and sharp corners. A level made flat stays flat-faced when a lower level is changed.

en: Live Mirror (across the world origin)
zh: Live Mirror (across the world origin)（实时镜像，沿世界原点）

en: Adds a live mirrored copy of the selected object across the world {name} plane. It is the same mesh drawn again, so sculpting, remeshing or cutting either side shows on both, and moving one moves the other. More planes can be ticked afterwards. Click a copy to work from its side.
zh: 沿世界 {name} 平面为所选物体添加一个实时镜像副本。它是同一个网格的再次绘制，所以雕刻、Remesh 或切割任何一侧都会在两侧同时显示，移动其中一个也会移动另一个。之后还可以勾选更多平面。点击某个副本即可从它那一侧操作。

en: A live copy: sculpt or move either side and the other follows.
zh: 实时副本：雕刻或移动任何一侧，另一侧都会跟随。

en: Mirror across the world {name} plane.
zh: 沿世界 {name} 平面镜像。

en: Turns the live mirror copies into real, separate objects, exactly where they are. Undo (Ctrl+Z) makes them live again.
zh: 把实时镜像副本变成真实、独立的物体，位置保持不变。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可让它们重新变为实时。

en: Removes the selected object's live mirror copies. Undo (Ctrl+Z) brings them back.
zh: 移除所选物体的实时镜像副本。撤销（Ctrl+Z）可找回。

en: Puts the gizmo on the object itself.
zh: 把 Gizmo 放在物体本身上。

en: Puts the gizmo on this copy: dragging it moves the object the other way. Clicking the copy in the viewport with the Transform tool does the same.
zh: 把 Gizmo 放在这个副本上：拖动它会让物体朝相反方向移动。用 Transform 工具在视口中点击该副本，效果相同。

en: Draw the inside of the surface too, tinted blue-grey. Off, where the model is open (a hole, a cut, hidden faces) you look straight through it to what is behind.
zh: 同时绘制表面的内侧，染成蓝灰色。关闭时，模型敞开的地方（孔洞、切口、隐藏的面）会让你直接看穿到后面。

en: Colour each face group of the model differently (Mesh tab, Face Groups card).
zh: 给模型的每个面组染上不同的颜色（Mesh 选项卡，Face Groups 卡片）。

en: Drag an arrow to move, a ring to turn, a cube on an arm to stretch that way, or the white cube to resize.\n\nClick another object to select it; Shift or Ctrl + click adds it to the selection or takes it out. Several selected objects go together, about their shared centre. Drag anywhere else to orbit.\n\nIf part of the object is masked, the gizmo reshapes the rest and leaves the masked part where it is.\n\nEdit Pivot moves and turns the gizmo itself (the object's pivot) and leaves the object where it is.
zh: 拖动箭头可移动，拖动圆环可旋转，拖动手臂上的方块可沿该方向拉伸，拖动白色方块可整体缩放。\n\n点击另一个物体可选中它；Shift 或 Ctrl + 点击可将其加入选择或取消选择。选中多个物体时，它们会绕共同的中心一起变换。在其他地方拖动可环绕视图。\n\n如果物体有一部分被遮罩，Gizmo 只改变其余部分，被遮罩的部分保持原位。\n\nEdit Pivot 移动和旋转的是 Gizmo 本身（即物体的轴心），物体保持原位。

en: Gizmo axes (arrows and rings)
zh: Gizmo axes (arrows and rings)（Gizmo 的轴：箭头和圆环）

en: {0} The scale cubes always follow the object's own axes.
zh: {0} 缩放方块始终跟随物体自身的轴。

en: Put the gizmo away and sculpt (G).
zh: 收起 Gizmo 并开始雕刻（G）。

en: Pivot (where the gizmo sits, and its axes)
zh: Pivot (where the gizmo sits, and its axes)（轴心：Gizmo 所在的位置及其轴）

en: While this is on, the gizmo moves and turns the object's pivot instead of the object: drag an arrow to move it, a ring to turn its axes, or click on the object to put the pivot on the surface there. The object stays where it is. The pivot is what the object turns and resizes about, and where its own (Local) symmetry planes pass, so those come with it. Each change is one undo step.
zh: 开启后，Gizmo 移动和旋转的是物体的轴心，而不是物体本身：拖动箭头可移动轴心，拖动圆环可旋转它的轴，或点击物体把轴心放到那里的表面上。物体保持原位。物体绕轴心旋转和缩放，它自己的（Local）对称平面也经过轴心，所以这些平面会跟着一起移动。每次改动算一个撤销步骤。

en: Drag an arrow or a ring to move or turn the pivot, or click on the object to put it there. The object stays put.
zh: 拖动箭头或圆环可移动或旋转轴心，或点击物体把轴心放到那里。物体保持不动。

en: Puts the pivot in the middle of the object, for a model that came in with it far away. The object stays where it is.
zh: 把轴心放到物体正中，适用于导入时轴心离得很远的模型。物体保持原位。

en: Turns the pivot's axes back to line up with the world's, for when they have got tipped over. The object stays where it is.
zh: 把轴心的轴转回与世界轴对齐，适用于它们被转歪的情况。物体保持原位。

zh: Step back ({0})（后退一步）

en: A tool takes the panel while it is up; Esc, its key again or picking a brush puts it away.
zh: 工具打开时会占用面板；按 Esc、再按一次它的键或选取笔刷，即可收起工具。

en: Brush adds material. Hold Ctrl or use the right button to flip.
zh: 笔刷添加材料。按住 Ctrl 或使用右键可反向操作。

en: Brush removes material.
zh: 笔刷去除材料。

en: Paint a mask that protects the surface from brushes (M). Ctrl erases.
zh: 绘制遮罩来保护表面不受笔刷影响（M）。Ctrl 擦除。

en: How far the surface follows the pen: at 100% it stays under it, lower is a gentler pull. Hold F and drag.
zh: 表面跟随笔的程度：100% 时它始终在笔下，数值越低牵引越柔和。按住 F 并拖动。

en: Flow: how much of the Opacity each dab of paint lays. Low builds paint up softly as you go over a place. Hold F and drag.
zh: Flow：每个笔点铺上多少 Opacity 的颜料。数值低时，反复涂抹同一处会让颜料柔和地累积。按住 F 并拖动。

en: How far the pulled clay follows the pen: at 100% and full pressure it stays with it; lower, or a lighter touch, lets it lag and thin out. Hold F and drag.
zh: 被拉出的泥跟随笔的程度：100% 且压力最大时它始终跟着笔；数值更低或下笔更轻，则会滞后并变细。按住 F 并拖动。

en: How hard the brush works. The slider gives the gentle end the most room: on Standard, Inflate and Layer one pass at 10% barely shows, at 50% it is a clear stroke and at 100% a strong one. Hold F and drag.
zh: 笔刷用力的程度。滑块把最多的空间留给了轻的一端：在 Standard、Inflate 和 Layer 上，10% 时一遍几乎看不出来，50% 时是明显的一笔，100% 时则很强。按住 F 并拖动。

en: Focal Shift (Hard - Soft)
zh: Focal Shift (Hard - Soft)（焦点偏移：硬 - 软）

en: Moves the brush's weight toward its centre or its edge. Hold D and drag.
zh: 把笔刷的力度重心移向中心或边缘。按住 D 并拖动。

en: How crisp the painted mask's edge is: low fades it out softly toward the rim of the ring, high masks the ring more evenly and faster.
zh: 绘制的遮罩边缘有多清晰：低则向笔刷圈边缘柔和淡出，高则更均匀、更快地遮罩整个圈。

en: Paint to protect. Ctrl or the right button erases.
zh: 涂抹以保护。Ctrl 或右键擦除。

en: Accumulate (Off)
zh: Accumulate (Off)（累积）

en: On: every pass over the same spot adds more, with no limit. Off: a stroke builds up to a height and no further, however often you go over the same place in it; lift the pen and stroke again to build on top.
zh: 开：每次经过同一处都会继续添加，没有上限。关：一笔最多累积到一定高度，无论在这一笔里反复经过同一处多少次都不会更高；抬笔后再画一笔才能继续往上叠加。

en: How fast the brush builds, on top of Strength: higher gets to the full effect in fewer passes. On Layer, how fast the step rises to its height.
zh: 在 Strength 之外，笔刷累积的速度：越高，用越少遍数就能达到完整效果。对 Layer 来说，则是台阶升到其高度的速度。

en: Front Facing Only (Off)
zh: Front Facing Only (Off)（仅正面）

en: Ignore surface that faces away from the camera, so thin shapes are not hit on the far side.
zh: 忽略背向相机的表面，这样薄的形状背面就不会被误伤。

en: How much clay a stroke lays on: with Accumulate off, the height it builds up to, as a share of the brush's size.
zh: 一笔铺上多少泥：关闭 Accumulate 时，是它累积到的高度，以笔刷大小的比例计。

en: Tip Softness (Flat - Domed)
zh: Tip Softness (Flat - Domed)（笔尖柔和度：平 - 圆顶）

en: The shape of the clay under the ring: low lays a flat-topped strip, high a rounded one.
zh: 笔刷圈下泥的形状：低则铺出平顶条带，高则铺出圆顶条带。

en: Smooths away the small ridges the dabs leave at the edge of the stroke, where the surface bends sharply, without wiping out what the stroke built. 0 leaves them.
zh: 抹平笔点在笔触边缘（表面急剧弯折处）留下的小脊线，同时不抹掉这一笔堆出来的内容。0 则保留它们。

en: How hard the sides of the cut are drawn in toward its line: higher is a tighter, sharper crease.
zh: 切口两侧向切线收拢的力度：越高，折痕越紧、越锐利。

en: How deep the crease cuts, for the brush's size.
zh: 折痕切得多深，相对于笔刷大小而言。

en: Plane Offset (Lower - Higher)
zh: Plane Offset (Lower - Higher)（平面偏移：低 - 高）

en: Moves the plane Flatten works to below or above the surface under the ring: lower cuts in, higher leaves a raised plateau.
zh: 把 Flatten 作用的平面移到笔刷圈下表面的下方或上方：低则切入，高则留下一块凸起的平台。

en: Draws the surface in to the line of the stroke. Ctrl or the right button spreads it apart.
zh: 把表面向笔触线收拢。Ctrl 或右键则向两侧推开。

en: Strength is how far the surface follows the pen: at 100% what is under the middle of the ring stays under it.
zh: Strength 是表面跟随笔的程度：100% 时，笔刷圈中心下的部分始终留在笔下。

en: How high the step Layer raises, as a share of the brush's size. A stroke never goes past it.
zh: Layer 抬起的台阶有多高，以笔刷大小的比例计。一笔绝不会超过它。

en: Grab only the surface joined to the point under the pointer: moving one finger leaves the one next to it alone.
zh: 只抓取与指针下那一点相连的表面：移动一根手指时，旁边那根不会动。

en: Ctrl or the right button pushes the surface in or pulls it out, the way it faces: drag up for out, down for in. At the outline, drag the way it faces.
zh: Ctrl 或右键沿表面朝向把它向内推或向外拉：向上拖是向外，向下拖是向内。在轮廓处，沿其朝向拖动。

en: Put every setting of this brush back as it was when you made it: strength, falloff, alpha,              dabs, pen pressure, Lazy Mouse and the rest. The brush's size is shared and stays as it is.
zh: 把这支笔刷的所有设置恢复成你创建它时的样子：强度、衰减、Alpha、笔点、压感、Lazy Mouse 等。笔刷大小是共用的，保持不变。

en: Put every setting of this brush back as it came with the app: strength, falloff, alpha, dabs,              pen pressure, Lazy Mouse and the rest. The brush's size is shared and stays as it is.
zh: 把这支笔刷的所有设置恢复成应用自带时的样子：强度、衰减、Alpha、笔点、压感、Lazy Mouse 等。笔刷大小是共用的，保持不变。

en: Rebuilds the surface with even triangles at this resolution. It keeps both sides as they are, whatever the symmetry. Use it after big shape changes, or to add detail (R).
zh: 按这个分辨率用均匀的三角面重建表面。无论对称如何设置，它都保持两侧原样。大幅改变形状之后，或想增加细节时使用（R）。

en: Rebuilds the surface as about this many even quads that follow the form. Export keeps the quads.
zh: 把表面重建成大约这么多个顺着形体走向的均匀四边面。导出时会保留四边面。

en: Masked areas are protected from every brush.
zh: 被遮罩的区域不受任何笔刷影响。

en: What is masked becomes free and what is free becomes masked. One undo step.
zh: 被遮罩的变成自由的，自由的变成被遮罩。算一个撤销步骤。

en: Takes the whole mask away. One undo step.

en: Spreads the mask out by one ring of points. One undo step.

en: Write every selected object into one Autodesk FBX, for Blender, Unity and the like: each an object of its own under its name, placed as it is in the scene, Y up, at real size (1 unit is 1 cm). A painted object goes with its colour, and each face with the material most of it is painted with. Ctrl + click rows of the Objects list to select more than one.
zh: 把所有选中的物体写入同一个 Autodesk FBX，供 Blender、Unity 等使用：每个物体以自己的名称独立存在，保持它在场景中的摆放，Y 轴向上，真实尺寸（1 个单位为 1 cm）。绘制过的物体会带上颜色，每个面带上占其大部分的材质。在 Objects 列表中按 Ctrl + 点击各行可选中多个。

en: PLY (painted colour)...
zh: PLY (painted colour)...（导出 PLY，带绘制颜色）

en: Write the selected object as a PLY, placed as it is in the scene: the format most programs read painted colour from (MeshLab, colour 3D printing). A painted object goes with its colour and, for each material it is painted with, how much of it is at each point.
zh: 把所选物体写成 PLY，保持它在场景中的摆放：这是大多数程序读取绘制颜色所用的格式（MeshLab、彩色 3D 打印）。绘制过的物体会带上颜色，以及它用到的每种材质在各点上的占比。

en: STL for Printing (mm)...
zh: STL for Printing (mm)...（导出 STL，用于打印）

en: Write the selected object as a binary STL for a slicer: placed as it is in the scene, upright (Z up), in millimetres at real size (1 unit is 10 mm). If the object has holes it says so first, and asks.
zh: 把所选物体写成供切片软件使用的二进制 STL：保持它在场景中的摆放，直立（Z 轴向上），以毫米为单位、真实尺寸（1 个单位为 10 mm）。如果物体有孔洞，会先提示并询问。

en: Export FBX also writes how much of each painted material is at each point into the object's UV sets 1 to 4 (two materials a set), for a game shader of your own to blend textures by. Unity reads them as uv2 to uv5. Off: the FBX has its colour and one material per face, which is all Blender and Unity use by themselves.
zh: Export FBX 还会把每种绘制材质在各点上的占比写入物体的 UV 集 1 到 4（每集两种材质），供你自己的游戏着色器据此混合贴图。Unity 会把它们读作 uv2 到 uv5。关闭时：FBX 只带颜色和每个面一种材质，这就是 Blender 和 Unity 自身所能用到的全部内容。

en: {picked} objects are selected. FBX writes them all into one file; OBJ, PLY and STL write the bright one only.
zh: 已选中 {picked} 个物体。FBX 会把它们全部写入同一个文件；OBJ、PLY 和 STL 只写入高亮的那个。

en: Shade with a picture of a lit sphere: quick, and the same from every angle.
zh: 用一张受光球体的图片着色：速度快，而且从任何角度看都一样。

en: Smoothing makes a form thinner every time: a finger, a fin's edge, a small ball. This gives that back. At 100% the form keeps its thickness while its bumps still go; at 0% it shrinks as it always has.
zh: 每次平滑都会让形体变细：手指、鳍的边缘、小球。这个选项把变细的部分补回来。100% 时形体保持厚度，凸起仍会被抹掉；0% 时则像往常一样缩小。

en: These are also what Shift + drag does with any other brush.
zh: 任何其他笔刷按住 Shift 并拖动时，做的也是这些。

en: Smooths the surface a little behind every dab, the way that keeps its volume, so the stroke leaves it clean: no lumps or spikes, and a swelling or a pull keeps its thickness. Leans on the pen like the brush does. At 0% nothing is smoothed.
zh: 在每个笔点之后稍微平滑一下表面，用的是保持体积的方式，所以笔触留下的表面很干净：没有疙瘩和尖刺，鼓起或拉出的部分保持厚度。对笔压的反应和笔刷一致。0% 时不做任何平滑。

en: Slides the mesh within the surface {when}, so the triangles the brush stretched come back even without the shape changing: cleaner for the next stroke. At 0% they stay stretched.
zh: 让网格在表面内滑动（时机：{when}），使笔刷拉伸过的三角面重新变均匀而形状不变：下一笔更干净。0% 时它们保持被拉伸的状态。

en: Thickness (Thin - Full)
zh: Thickness (Thin - Full)（粗细：细 - 满）

en: How a pull keeps its thickness. Higher keeps a long pull as thick at its tip as where it started; 0 lets it thin out as it goes; below 0 draws it in to a point, for horns and spikes.
zh: 拉出的部分如何保持粗细。越高，长长拉出的尖端和起点一样粗；0 则让它越拉越细；低于 0 则收成一个尖，适合犄角和尖刺。

en: Keeps the level the brush works to steady along the stroke, so it does not tip and bob over every bump the brush passes: a smoother, more even strip. At 0% each dab finds its own level; at 100% the stroke keeps the one it started with.
zh: 让笔刷作用的水平面沿笔触保持稳定，不会在经过的每个凸起上倾斜、起伏：条带更平滑、更均匀。0% 时每个笔点各自找自己的水平面；100% 时整笔保持起笔时的那一个。

en: Evens out the directions Inflate pushes the surface in, so neighbouring bits swell together. Higher keeps a swelling smooth over sharp or rough forms; at 0% every point pushes straight out its own way.
zh: 让 Inflate 推动表面的方向更均匀，使相邻的部分一起鼓起。越高，鼓起在尖锐或粗糙的形体上越平滑；0% 时每个点都沿自己的方向直直向外推。

en: Lock Plane (Off)
zh: Lock Plane (Off)（锁定平面）

en: On: the plane is fixed where the stroke begins, and the whole stroke levels to that one plane, for cutting a true flat facet. It works on what lies within half the brush's size of the plane and leaves the rest alone. Off: the plane follows the surface under the ring.
zh: 开：平面固定在起笔处，整笔都抹平到这同一个平面上，用来切出真正平整的切面。它只作用于离平面不超过笔刷大小一半的部分，其余不动。关：平面跟随笔刷圈下的表面。

en: Switch between perspective and orthographic (Numpad 5). An axis view goes orthographic by itself, and back to perspective when you turn the view by hand.

en: The lens the view is seen through. Wide (18-24 mm) bends the model toward you, as a camera held close does; 50 mm is about what the eye sees; 85 mm and up flatten it, as portraits are taken. The model stays the size it is on screen.
zh: 观察视图所用的镜头。广角（18-24 mm）会让模型向你这边透视变形，就像相机靠得很近时那样；50 mm 大致相当于人眼所见；85 mm 及以上会让模型显得更扁平，就像拍人像一样。模型在屏幕上的大小保持不变。

en: Puts the detail of another object onto this one. For clean topology: Clone the sculpt, Quad Remesh the copy, then Subdivide (Ctrl+D) and Project, a few times over. en: How much Remesh irons out. Off copies the old surface exactly, sharpest and quickest. A little takes out the jagged edges along grooves, and the old mesh's facets that come back when the resolution goes up. More smooths further, and softens the finest lines. Remesh takes a little longer above Off.
zh: Remesh 抹平的程度。Off 会原样复制旧表面，最锐利也最快。少量会去掉凹槽边缘的锯齿，以及分辨率提高后重新出现的旧网格小平面。更多则抹得更平，并让最细的线条变柔和。高于 Off 时，Remesh 会稍慢一点。

zh: Off：Remesh 能做到的最锐利效果。

en: Light: irons out jagged groove edges and old facets.

zh: Strong：更平滑；最细的线条会变柔和。

en: The skin does not follow the spheres afterwards: change them and you need a new skin, so settle the overall shape before sculpting detail.

en: Tap to put this message away. It stays on the status line of the Scene panel.
zh: 轻点可收起这条消息。它会保留在 Scene 面板的状态栏里。

en: How much weaker the brush gets as you press more lightly. At 100% a feather touch does next to nothing; at 0% pressure does not change the strength at all. Full pressure is always the Strength set above, and so is a mouse. Most brushes start at 100%.
zh: 下笔越轻，笔刷变弱多少。100% 时，羽毛般的轻触几乎不起作用；0% 时压力完全不改变强度。压力最大时始终是上面设定的 Strength，鼠标也是如此。大多数笔刷默认是 100%。

en: How far apart the brush lays its dabs, as a share of its size. Low is a smooth stroke; higher shows the dabs; from about 100% they stand apart, which is how to lay an alpha as separate prints. It changes how smooth the stroke is, not how much it builds.
zh: 笔刷铺下笔点的间距，以其大小的比例计。低则笔触平滑；越高越能看出一个个笔点；大约从 100% 起笔点会彼此分开，这就是把 Alpha 铺成单独印记的办法。它改变的是笔触的平滑程度，而不是累积的多少。

en: Throws each dab off the line of the stroke, any way along the surface, by a random distance up to this share of the brush's size. 0 is off.
zh: 让每个笔点随机偏离笔触的线，沿表面朝任意方向，偏移距离最多为笔刷大小的这个比例。0 为关闭。

en: Makes each dab smaller than the brush by a random amount, up to this much. 0 is off.
zh: 让每个笔点比笔刷随机小一些，最多小这么多。0 为关闭。

en: How much smaller the brush gets as you press more lightly, so a stroke can swell and taper. At 100% the lightest touch is a fine point; full pressure is always the size of the ring. 0 is off: pressure does not change the size. A mouse is always full size.
zh: 下笔越轻，笔刷变小多少，让一笔可以有粗有细。100% 时，最轻的触碰只是一个细点；压力最大时始终是笔刷圈的大小。0 为关闭：压力不改变大小。鼠标始终是完整大小。

en: Lazy Mouse (this brush)
zh: Lazy Mouse (this brush)（仅此笔刷）

en: The brush trails the pointer on a short rope, steadying long curves. Each brush keeps its own: on for a line brush such as Dam Standard, off for Move, say. Move and Pose never trail.
zh: 笔刷像拴在短绳上一样拖在指针后面，让长曲线更稳。每支笔刷各自保留设置：比如 Dam Standard 这类线条笔刷开启，Move 则关闭。Move 和 Pose 永远不会拖尾。

en: How long the rope is, in screen pixels: how far the pointer moves before the brush starts to follow. Longer steadies more, and the brush lags further behind.
zh: 绳子有多长，以屏幕像素计：指针移动多远后笔刷才开始跟随。越长越稳，笔刷也落后得越远。

en: How readily the brush follows once the rope is taut: at 1 it keeps up at the rope's length; lower lets it drift further behind and eases it round bends.
zh: 绳子绷紧后笔刷跟随得多积极：1 时它始终保持在绳长的距离上跟着；越低，它落得越远，转弯时也更圆滑。

en: Keep adding while the pointer rests in one place. For every brush.
zh: 指针停在一处时继续添加。对所有笔刷生效。

en: How the pen answers your hand, for every brush. Higher needs a firmer press before pressure counts, which steadies light strokes; lower answers to the lightest touch. What pressure then changes (size, strength, and by how much) is set for each brush, in its own settings.
zh: 笔对你手上力度的反应，对所有笔刷生效。越高，需要按得更重压力才起作用，这会让轻的笔触更稳；越低，最轻的触碰也有反应。压力具体改变什么（大小、强度，以及幅度）在每支笔刷自己的设置里设定。

en: No pen pressure has arrived yet since GolemSculpt opened. Press the pen on the tablet: this line turns into a live bar. If it stays, tick "Enable Windows Ink" in the Huion tablet driver, or the pen works as a mouse: full strength, full size.
zh: 自 GolemSculpt 打开以来，还没有收到任何笔压。请把笔按在数位板上：这一行会变成实时的进度条。如果它一直不变，请在 Huion 数位板驱动中勾选“Enable Windows Ink”，否则笔会被当成鼠标使用：满强度、满大小。

en: How many times each stroke is repeated around the axis.
zh: 每一笔绕轴重复多少次。

en: Repeats strokes around an axis you set below: typed in, or taken from the view.

en: Points the custom axis straight down the current view, through {whose} centre: seen from here, the repeats land evenly around it.

en: For a selected object whose two sides no longer match. Check Symmetry counts the vertices that pair up across the plane. Make Symmetric moves one side onto the mirror image of the other and keeps every triangle and the mask; Cut & Mirror throws one side away and rebuilds it as the mirror image of the other. Symmetry Cleanup puts the vertices near the plane exactly on it.
zh: 适用于两侧已不再吻合的所选物体。Check Symmetry 会统计隔着平面能配成对的顶点数。Make Symmetric 把一侧移到另一侧的镜像位置，并保留所有三角面和遮罩；Cut & Mirror 丢弃一侧，并把它重建为另一侧的镜像。Symmetry Cleanup 把靠近平面的顶点精确放到平面上。

en: The buttons below work across the object's own {0} plane: the one Mirror {1} sculpts across in Local space.
zh: 下面的按钮作用于物体自己的 {0} 平面：也就是 Mirror {1} 在 Local 空间中所沿的那个平面。

en: Says on the status line how many of the selected object's vertices pair up across the plane, and how many have no partner. Changes nothing.
zh: 在状态栏中说明所选物体有多少个顶点隔着平面配成了对，有多少个没有对应点。不会改变任何东西。

en: Moves the {moved} side's vertices onto the mirror image of the {copied} side, each onto its own partner across the object's own {0} plane. No triangle changes. With a mask painted only the unmasked part moves. Where too much of the model has no partner nothing is changed and the status line says so: Cut & Mirror is for that. One undo step (Ctrl+Z).
zh: 把 {moved} 一侧的顶点移到 {copied} 一侧的镜像位置，每个顶点移到它在物体自己的 {0} 平面另一侧的对应点上。三角面不会变。画了遮罩时，只有未遮罩的部分会移动。如果模型上没有对应点的部分太多，则不会改变任何东西，状态栏会提示：这种情况请用 Cut & Mirror。算一个撤销步骤（Ctrl+Z）。

en: Keeps the {kept} side of the selected object and rebuilds its {rebuilt} side as the exact mirror image of it, across the object's own {0} plane. What was on the {rebuilt} side is thrown away. The mask comes across; hidden parts are cut too and show again. One undo step (Ctrl+Z).
zh: 保留所选物体的 {kept} 一侧，并沿物体自己的 {0} 平面，把它的 {rebuilt} 一侧重建为前者的精确镜像。原来在 {rebuilt} 一侧的内容会被丢弃。遮罩会被带过来；隐藏的部分也会被切割，并重新显示。算一个撤销步骤（Ctrl+Z）。

en: Puts the vertices that sit close to the plane exactly on it, and merges the doubled ones that leaves: two halves joined along the plane become one surface. Neither side is moved as a whole. One undo step (Ctrl+Z).
zh: 把靠近平面的顶点精确放到平面上，并合并由此产生的重叠顶点：沿平面拼在一起的两半会成为一个表面。两侧都不会整体移动。算一个撤销步骤（Ctrl+Z）。

en: These work across the object's own plane, not the world's.
zh: 这些操作作用于物体自己的平面，而不是世界平面。

en: How far apart two vertices may be and still count as a mirrored pair, from tight to loose. Widen it if halves that were built separately are not matching up. For Cut & Mirror it is how near the plane a vertex must be to be kept as part of the seam.
zh: 两个顶点最远相距多少仍算作一对镜像点，从严到宽。如果分别建出来的两半对不上，请调宽。对 Cut & Mirror 来说，它是顶点离平面多近才会被保留为接缝的一部分。

en: Gap between peg and socket wall, per side. 0.1-0.2 mm is a snug fit on most FDM printers; resin wants less, a loose slip fit more. The socket is the peg's exact outline grown by this much.
zh: 销钉与销孔壁之间的间隙（每侧）。对大多数 FDM 打印机，0.1-0.2 mm 是紧密配合；树脂打印需要更小，松配合则需要更大。销孔就是把销钉的精确轮廓向外扩大这么多。

en: How much deeper the socket is than the peg is tall, so the peg never bottoms out and the faces close fully.

en: Uses fewer vertices on rings near the axis so every triangle is about the same size - no slivers at the poles, which brushes handle much better.
zh: 在靠近轴的环上使用更少的顶点，让每个三角面大小大致相同——两极处不会出现细长三角面，笔刷处理起来要好得多。

en: Turns on radial symmetry round the lathe's axis when the object is created, so every stroke on it is repeated all the way round. Set how many repeats under Symmetry in the sculpting panel. Undoing the Create puts the symmetry back as it was.

zh: 场数据与它的网格不匹配。

zh: 网格关于那个平面并不对称。

en: That grid has too many samples. Try a larger cell size.
zh: 这个网格的采样点太多了。试试更大的单元格大小。
